- **view.cs**: Jak v AutoCADu otočit pohled příkazem POHLED (VIEW, -POHLED): šest pravoúhlých pohledů a čtyři izometrické a USS, který nastaví každý pravoúhlý. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.cs.md))
- **view.de**: So wechselt AutoCADs AUSSCHNT (VIEW, -VIEW) in die sechs orthogonalen und die vier isometrischen Ansichten und setzt das BKS jeder Ansicht. Online testen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.de.md))
- **view.es**: Cómo funciona VISTA (VIEW, alias VI) de AutoCAD en español: las seis vistas ortogonales, las cuatro isométricas y el SCP que fija cada ortogonal. Pruébalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.es.md))
- **view.export-plans-to-pdf**: Export sheet plans (layouts) in the project to PDF — one combined file or one per plan — optionally filtered by a plan-name regex. The "issue the floor-plan pack" primitive. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/allplan-2025/commands/view.export-plans-to-pdf.md) · ★7)
- **view.fr**: Comment VUE (VIEW, alias VU, -VUE) d'AutoCAD passe aux six vues orthogonales et aux quatre isométriques, et le SCU que fixe chaque vue orthogonale. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.fr.md))
- **view.h**: - **Source path:** `source/src/include/commands/view.h` - **Lines:** 25 - **Last verified commit:** `ef6a95c7c64` ([matejformanek/postgres-claude](https://github.com/matejformanek/postgres-claude/blob/HEAD/knowledge/files/src/include/commands/view.h.md) · ★2)
- **view.hu**: Így fordít az AutoCAD NÉZET (VIEW, N, -NÉZET) parancsa a hat ortografikus és a négy izometrikus nézetre, és milyen FKR-t állít be az ortografikus. Próbálja ki! ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.hu.md))
- **view.it**: Come VISTA (VIEW, V, -VIEW) di AutoCAD passa alle sei viste ortogonali e alle quattro assonometriche, e quale UCS imposta ogni vista ortogonale. Provalo online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.it.md))
- **view.ja**: AutoCAD の VIEW[ビュー管理](V、-VIEW)で 6 つの直交投影ビューと 4 つのアイソメ ビューに切り替える方法と、直交投影ビューが設定する UCS を解説。オンラインで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.ja.md))
- **view.ko**: AutoCAD VIEW(V, -VIEW)로 여섯 가지 정투영 뷰와 네 가지 등각투영 뷰로 뷰를 돌리는 방법, 그리고 정투영 뷰가 설정하는 UCS를 설명합니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.ko.md))
- **view.pl**: Jak w AutoCADzie obrócić widok poleceniem WIDOK (VIEW, -WIDOK): sześć widoków prostokątnych i cztery izometryczne oraz LUW ustawiany przez każdy prostokątny. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.pl.md))
- **view.pt-BR**: Como o VIEW do AutoCAD em português, VISTA (alias V), gira para as seis vistas ortogonais e as quatro isométricas, e o UCS de cada ortogonal. Teste grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.pt-BR.md))
- **view.publish-set**: Publish a Publisher Set to its configured output — PDF sheet pack, DWG batch, BIMx package, IFC export — whatever format the set is configured for. - **view.ru**: Как в AutoCAD повернуть вид командой ВИД (VIEW, -ВИД): шесть ортогональных и четыре изометрических вида и ПСК, которую задает каждый ортогональный. Попробуйте. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.ru.md))
- **view.zh-CN**: AutoCAD VIEW（快捷键 V、-VIEW）怎么切换到六个正交视图和四个等轴测视图，以及每个正交视图设置的 UCS。在线免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.zh-CN.md))

- **vision-adjust.template**: This prompt was authored for Claude-style slash workflows. In Codex runtime, adapt tool calls as follows: - `AskUserQuestion` =&gt; ask the user directly in chat. - `WebSearch`/`WebFetch` =&gt; use available web tools (`search_query`, `open`, `find`) and cite links. - `Read`/`Write` =&gt; use shell/filesystem tools in this workspace. - Claude-only MCP calls (for example Playwright MCP names) =&gt; use availab ([evan043/claude-cli-advanced-starter-pack](https://github.com/evan043/claude-cli-advanced-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/templates/commands/vision-adjust.template.md) · ★62)
- **vision-board-build.contract**: Use `--repo &lt;path&gt;` for a Git target or `--workspace &lt;path&gt;` for a new-idea workspace. Both inputs are explicit readable files, resolved from the caller's directory. The draft is nonempty UTF-8 Markdown. Proposals conform to `skills/vision/schemas/vision-proposals.json`: ([knowttl/bstack](https://github.com/knowttl/bstack/blob/HEAD/runtime/scripts/commands/vision-board-build.contract.md))
- **vision-board-launch.contract**: Both commands also accept --repo for a Git target. Resume uses the explicit board path from an earlier build, preserving its original draft and proposals. The manifest target, original exact-byte draft hash and ordered proposal IDs must still agree. A different target or changed draft fails with incompatible-board before launch or new scratch creation. Missing board files or inaccessible resources ([knowttl/bstack](https://github.com/knowttl/bstack/blob/HEAD/runtime/scripts/commands/vision-board-launch.contract.md))
- **vision-board-verdicts.contract**: Verdicts takes the complete-round Context data JSON embedded in the terminal's vision-verdict prompt. Copy that JSON unchanged to a scratch file, retaining the captured terminal response as provenance. The [vision-verdicts schema source](../../schemas/vision-verdicts.json) defines the input fields and accepted verdict labels: ([knowttl/bstack](https://github.com/knowttl/bstack/blob/HEAD/runtime/scripts/commands/vision-board-verdicts.contract.md))
